2233. In order for rocky planets to keep water for more than a few 100 million years they have to maintain a molten core. To do this they need a higher ratio of radioactive isotopes than is normal in planet formation. That is why every other rocky planet in our system have long since lost their magnetic fields. Earth had a one in billions collision with Thera that added its molten core and radioactive isotopes to ours. It's very unlikely planets in TRAPPIST-1 or any other system have these conditions. Our Earth is one of the greatest treasures in the Universe.

2746. Statistically there is usually a big difference between the properties of a typical individual and the properties of a typical group. In other words, the majority of data is contained within the biggest groups, but the majority of groups, have a smaller amount of data. For example, the majority of humans live in countries with populations over 180 million, but the majority of countries have population under 6 million. Therefore, if you knew you were a human, but you didn’t know where you are from, you can assume you are from a country with over 180 million people, despite this being a small amount of counties. Similar to this, if you didn’t know your blood type, which most people don’t and I told you that 70% of the population is made up of A+ and O+ blood types, you would assume you have type A+ or O+ blood. However, if I just told you that there were 8 blood types, you would expect that each would have around 17% of the population.   So as humans we can expect, just like someone who doesn’t know their blood type, that we are similar to the majority of other life forms. However, just like countries, we can assume that the majority of intelligent lifeforms live on a small amount of planets. Therefore, there are less intelligent life habiting planets that are like ours, than not. Using this, we can assume that most planets with extra-terrestrial life have a smaller population than us, and this tells us a lot about what we should be looking for.  So basically, NASA, You're doing it wrong

What we need is a gravity propulsion system that can distort the fabric of spacetime. If we have a spaceship that can manipulate space and time, this makes interstellar travel possible, since any occupants onboard such a ship would be be surrounded by an intense gravitational field and would experience no time elapsing even after travelling many light years. 3208. anyone who studies science knows this is mostly made up.  The only way they know about these planets is because their orbit dims the starlight when the planet is in transit.  The dimming of the light allows the scientists to make a guesstimate on where the planet is but even that is not 100 percent accurate.  On top of that the light from the star blocks scientists from studying the spectral lines emitted from the so called planets so there is no way to know the true make up of the planet.  All this is just pure speculation which makes for great news even though it can be called fake news.

3554. In today's news, the prominent headline on Business Insider read "Astronomers have found 7 Earth-size planets circling a dwarf star — and some might be able to host life". (http://www.businessinsider.com/earth-like-planets-seven-trappist-1-system-2017-02) It states that TRAPPIST-1, a red dwarf star 39 light years away, is orbited by at least 7 earth-like planets which are habitable and possibly --maybe even likely-- host life. That is spectacular.   But, allow me to share something even more spectacular. God says in the Qur'an:  (اللَّهُ الَّذِي خَلَقَ سَبْعَ سَمَاوَاتٍ وَمِنَ الْأَرْضِ مِثْلَهُنَّ يَتَنَزَّلُ الْأَمْرُ بَيْنَهُنَّ لِتَعْلَمُوا أَنَّ اللَّهَ عَلَىٰ كُلِّ شَيْءٍ قَدِيرٌ وَأَنَّ اللَّهَ قَدْ أَحَاطَ بِكُلِّ شَيْءٍ عِلْمًا)  "God is the One who created several celestial firmaments and, in proportion, several earths.He exerts His Command through them all so that you might know that God has power over all things, and that God surrounds all things in His omniscience." [Qur'an 65:12]  Ibn `Abbas said about this verse in a hair-raising narration:  (لو حدثتكم بتفسيرها لكفرتم وكفركم تكذيبكم بها) "If I told you the true meaning of this verse, you would disbelieve. And your disbelief would be the rejection of it."  In another chilling narration he says:  (ما يؤمنك أن أخبرك بها فتكفر) "I do not feel safe informing you of its meaning lest you should commit disbelief."  But, it seems that Ibn `Abbas eventually did give in and tell a little...and it is no less astounding:  (سبع أرضين ، في كل أرض نبي كنبيكم ، وآدم كآدم ، ونوح كنوح ، وإبراهيم ، كإبراهيم ، وعيسى كعيسى) "Several earths, and in every earth is a Prophet like your Prophet, an Adam like Adam, a Noah like Noah, an Abraham like Abraham, a Jesus like Jesus.."  So, according to this narration of Ibn `Abbas as related by Imam al-Bayhaqi, these other worlds have their own Prophets just like we do! And, these narrations puzzled the Islamic scholars of earlier eras so much that the great 15th Century theologian Jalal al-Din al-Suyuti said about the above narration that just because a narration's chain of transmission (isnad) is established (sahih), that doesn't mean it's content (matn) must be established (sahih). And that became one of his oft-cited principles in Hadith.  And today with quantum theory and parallel universes, the concept of "An Adam like Adam, a Noah like Noah, an Abraham like Abraham.." becomes even more complex with its scientific possibilities. Do we know that the Qur'an is talking about any of these things? Of course not. It may refer to the things I have mentioned, or even something not yet discovered. It may refer to realities that may never be discovered!   But what we absolutely do know is that the Qur'an told us nearly 1500 years ago what science is scratching the surface of today:   There is, indeed, intelligent life in other worlds out there in the great beyond. So when you stare at the stars in the night's sky remember:  (اللَّهُ الَّذِي خَلَقَ سَبْعَ سَمَاوَاتٍ وَمِنَ الْأَرْضِ مِثْلَهُنَّ يَتَنَزَّلُ الْأَمْرُ بَيْنَهُنَّ لِتَعْلَمُوا أَنَّ اللَّهَ عَلَىٰ كُلِّ شَيْءٍ قَدِيرٌ وَأَنَّ اللَّهَ قَدْ أَحَاطَ بِكُلِّ شَيْءٍ عِلْمًا)  "God is the One who created several celestial firmaments and, in proportion, several earths.He exerts His Command through them all so that you might know that God has power over all things, and that God surrounds all things in His omniscience." [Qur'an 65:12]  ~  Shibli Zaman

3755. Nasa is so going to go down in history as part of  ALL THAT IS WRONG WITH THIS PLANET! This is misdirection at a time of obvious interplanetary activity. Like a Magician get them looking the wrong way!  And in any case ...40 Light Years away ! Bottom line: How far is a light-year? It’s the distance a beam of light travels in one year – a distance of six trillion miles. 40 X 6 Trillion equals     240 Trillion Miles away   The main reason for using light years, however, is because the distances we deal with in space are immense. If we stick to miles or kilometers we quickly run into unwieldy numbers just measuring the distance to the nearest star: a dim red dwarf called Proxima Centauri that sits a mere 24,000,000,000,000 miles away! Using a longer yard stick, so to speak, helps keep the numbers at least manageable. To an astronomer, the distance to Proxima Centauri is only four light years. Put another way, it takes the light from our stellar next door neighbour four years to get to Earth.

I'm not saying that it's justification for war - it would certainly be nice if we all got along, but war has played a part in the advancement of the human race. 4822. While this may seem like a "groundbreaking discovery" (it's not, we have found similar numbers of "rocky planets" around other stars), we as humans tend to use the "habitable zone" theory selfishly. The fact is, most Earth-like life that exists in the Milky Way may not even inbabit planets, but instead large moons around Jupiter or Saturn-sized bodies. Many of those worlds would lay outside of the Habitable Zone.   So a "Habitable Zone" is really quite useless unless the only possible life we are interested in is on "large planets like our own".

4869. Great discovery, thanks to all. I wish you will continue to have funding to keep up with this high standard of work. Just one comment about the rendering in the video: planets which are tidally locked, as very probable for this system, are subject to significant gradients of temperature between the illuminated side and the dark side. Consequently their atmosphere, if present, is subject to never ending strong winds if not everlasting storms. A video showing still clouds and calm waters is a representation of a very unlikely scenario. The Earth is not tidally locked, that is why everyone of us has the chance to enjoy days with little or no wind. For an average alien, Earth would be characterized as ""Quiet" due to its mild atmospheric events... Earth is a silent planet, its winds are not screaming in our ears all the time :)

5372. The only real facts about these "planets" are the scientists looked at a particular star in the sky, observed dimming at regular intervals. The assumption is planets. LEDs actually flicker at a known interval, we call a hertz cycle. But it could just be tiny orbiting bodies of dark matter surrounding the LED causing what we perceive as the flicker. 5588. To put this in perspective: Our solar system only has 4 rocky planets, and only 2 of those are even in the water-favoring "habitable zone" (and Mars barely counts).  Venus and Mercury are too hot.  A system with 7 planets increases the odds that at least ONE will have life.  Of course, TRAPPIST-1 is a cold star, so even the worlds in the habitable zone might have tidal lock issues (only one side facing the star, and/or weird and irregular day/night cycles when they're present).  But still, the odds look better than average now.  Oh, and with the worlds being as close as they are?  This would be a test to see if panspermia is a thing: if signs of life can spread from one world to several nearby.  If multiple worlds have life AND are right next to each other, it would indicate a spread, versus two worlds farther apart living (for example) under different atmospheres.  (of course I can't be the only one who wants SETI to cut to the chase and scan the daylights out of TRAPPIST-1 now for radio-frequency signals, but hey, first things first.  any signals "they" might receive from us would be no more recent than 1978, due to distance. . . )
